# Acquisition inventory for 679 technologies

## Contents

- [How to read](#how-to-read)
- [Technologies](#technologies)

## How to read

Each row gives the source weight, starting status, number of distinct option / grant / progress sources and the names and game IDs of all published sources. Groups with several sources expand inline. The technology name links to its game definition. “Conditional start” indicates starting_potential. “×0” indicates a top-level or unconditional zero factor. “Debris” marks an identified carrier requiring validation. An open route or recipient issue is not proof of a player grant. The JSON contains complete source lists, diagnostics, and AND/OR prerequisites. [Technology acquisition and readability audit](<TECHNOLOGY_ACQUISITION_AUDIT_EN.md>) · [JSON](<../../../../TMP/WEB_STTV/technology-audit/reports/audit.json>)
